Abstract: Although multi-label k-nearest neighbor (ML-kNN) is able to effectively solve multi-label learning (MLL) problem with local neighborhood similarity, its time complexity is nearly unacceptable with large-scale datasets. To solve this issue, we propose a novel ML-kNN algorithm with quantum computing techniques, which called quantum multi-label k-nearest neighbor (QML-kNN). In particular, we first accelerate the calculation of the prior probability by taking advantage of quantum phase estimation and Grover's amplitude amplification. Then, a controlled-SWAP test and a quantum k-maximal similarity search are used for efficiently identifying the neighbors. Subsequently, a quantum parallel counting circuit (QPCC) is designed to rapidly calculate the posterior probabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that QML-kNN is able to significantly reduce the time complexity of solving multi-label problems with performance improvement, achieving a substantial speedup over the classical MLL algorithm.

</details>


### [95] [Proposal for Estimating the Energy Gap of the Transverse-Field Ising Hamiltonian Using a D-Wave Quantum Annealer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21940)
*Kota Yamada,Yuichiro Matsuzaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用D-Wave量子退火器，通过施加三角波振荡磁场诱导Rabi振荡，来估计横场Ising模型中基态与激发态之间的能隙，突破了传统量子退火仅限于基态搜索的限制，并通过小规模系统数值模拟验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算在大尺度或高维横场Ising模型中受限于希尔伯特空间的指数增长，无法有效计算基态和激发态；传统量子退火方法也局限于基态搜索，难以获取激发态信息。

Method: 在D-Wave量子退火器上施加三角波振荡磁场，诱导Rabi振荡，通过振荡行为估计基态与激发态之间的能隙。

Result: 通过小规模系统的数值模拟，验证了该方法能够有效估计能隙，且具备扩展到更大系统的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于量子器件的模拟提供了新途径，能够直接访问激发态信息，有望应用于更大规模系统。

Abstract: The transverse-field Ising model is a fundamental quantum spin system that captures the competition between quantum fluctuations and interactions, playing a central role in studies of quantum phase transitions and non-equilibrium dynamics. However, classical computations of ground and excited states in large-scale or high-dimensional systems are severely limited by the exponential growth of the Hilbert space. Here, we propose a novel approach using a D-Wave quantum annealer, where a triangular-wave oscillating magnetic field is applied to induce Rabi oscillations, allowing the estimation of energy gaps between the ground and excited states. Unlike conventional quantum annealing methods limited to ground-state searches, this approach can directly access excited-state information. It is potentially applicable to larger systems, providing a new avenue for quantum-device-based simulation. The validity of the method is demonstrated through numerical simulations of relatively small systems.

</details>


### [96] [QC-PHAST Search: Classical--Quantum Query Benchmarks for Finite-Pool Rare-Regime Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21995)
*Harsh Milind Tirhekar,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QC-PHAST是一个用于参数化动力系统中稀有状态发现的证据门控决策协议与查询核算框架，通过量子经典混合的搜索策略比较，帮助研究人员判断何时量子查询参考模型有价值，何时经典或资源感知搜索更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动力系统中稀有状态的发现是一个主动搜索问题，需要在候选参数稀少、非凸或碎片化的情况下找到经过验证的定性阈值交叉点。现有方法缺乏一个可审计的协议来判断不同搜索策略（方程感知搜索、标量分数主动搜索、谓词搜索等）的适用性及其与量子查询参考模型的比较。

Method: 提出QC-PHAST协议，包含：(1) 证据门控决策协议，利用科学元数据和先导证据评估四种搜索模式（方程感知、标量分数主动搜索、谓词搜索、查询模型比较）的可接受性；(2) 量子行采用Grover/BBHT未知M标记集查询作为参考（非新量子搜索定理或硬件加速声明）；(3) 通过直接边界构造、几何控制、在线模拟器循环和学习标签核算来识别经典结构、假阳性、校准成本或状态准备何时消除查询模型差距。

Result: 生成一个状态映射（regime map），明确了在何种条件下经典结构、假阳性、校准成本或状态准备会消除查询模型的边际优势，从而决定何时有限池标记集参考具有信息量，何时经典或资源感知搜索应占主导。

Conclusion: QC-PHAST是一个可审计的协议框架，用于判断在参数化动力系统稀有状态发现任务中，量子查询模型（Grover/BBHT）是否具有参考价值，以及何时经典搜索策略更为合适。该协议不声称量子硬件加速，而是提供了一个结构化的决策流程。

Abstract: Rare-regime discovery in parameterized dynamical systems is an active-search problem: find one verified parameter at which a scientifically defined qualitative threshold is crossed, even when acceptable candidates are rare, nonconvex, or fragmented. We introduce Quantum-Classical Phase-space and Stability-Threshold Search (QC-PHAST), an evidence-gated decision protocol and query-accounting framework for finite candidate libraries. A candidate induces a dynamical object, simulator-derived criticality score, and verified first-hit predicate. Scientific metadata and charged pilot evidence are used to assess whether equation-aware search, scalar-score active search, predicate-only search, or only a query-model comparison is admissible. The quantum row is the inherited Grover/Boyer--Brassard--Hoyer--Tapp (BBHT) unknown-$M$ marked-set query reference; it is not a new quantum-search theorem, materialized circuit, or hardware-speedup claim. The result is a regime map. Direct boundary constructions, geometry controls, online simulator loops, and learned-label accounting further identify when classical structure, false positives, calibration cost, or state preparation erases the query-model margin. QC-PHAST is therefore an auditable protocol for deciding when a finite-pool marked-set reference is informative and when classical or resource-aware search should dominate.

</details>


### [97] [Optical time travel: proposal for testing Hawking's Chronology Protection Conjecture in an optical analogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22056)
*D. Bermudez,U. Leonhardt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个光学实验方案来检验霍金的时序保护猜想，将时间旅行与激光发射联系起来，并指出激光阈值以上的放大真空涨落构成了时间旅行的量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验霍金提出的量子力学最终会阻止时间旅行的猜想（时序保护猜想）。

Method: 提出一个可行光学实验方案，将时间旅行与激光发射过程内在关联起来。

Result: 激光阈值以上的放大真空涨落建立了时间旅行的量子极限。

Conclusion: 该方案可用于实验检验霍金的时序保护猜想。

Abstract: Classical general relativity allows time travel, but Hawking [Phys. Rev. D 46, 603 (1992)] conjectured that quantum mechanics ultimately prevents it. Here we propose a feasible experiment to test Hawking's Chronology Protection Conjecture in optics. In this scheme, time travel is inherently related to lasing, and the amplified vacuum fluctuations above laser threshold establish the quantum limits of time travel

</details>


### [98] [Weak Permanent Anti-Concentration for Random Gaussian Matrices in Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22088)
*Fei Meng,Bin Cheng,Jianan Li,Man-Hong Yung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了高斯永久式（Gaussian permanent）的弱反集中性（weak anti-concentration bound），证明了随机高斯永久式以极高概率不会比其标准差超指数级小。这一结果填补了玻色采样（boson sampling）经典硬度证明中的关键数学空白，并结合Aaronson-Arkhipov框架表明：若剩余猜想成立，则超指数精度内经典模拟玻色采样将导致多项式层级崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样的经典硬度依赖于两个尚未严格证明的猜想：高斯永久式的平均情况难解性猜想和永久式反集中猜想（PACC）。其中PACC是关于随机高斯矩阵永久式分布性质的纯数学断言，此前仅对离散随机矩阵有结论，而线性光网络中的复高斯情形尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过上界约束随机高斯永久式比其标准差超指数级小的概率，建立了弱反集中界（weak anti-concentration bound）。这一方法不直接证明原PACC，但若将该界收紧至逆多项式分数，即可证明原猜想。

Result: 建立了高斯永久式的典型量级（typical magnitude），与Tao和Vu关于伯努利矩阵的经典结论相当；证明了高斯永久式满足弱反集中性质，即其值不会以过高概率远小于标准差。

Conclusion: 本结果为玻色采样经典硬度证明补全了关键一环：结合Aaronson-Arkhipov框架，若剩余猜想成立，则在超指数总变分距离内经典模拟玻色采样将导致多项式层级崩溃，进一步巩固了量子计算优势的理论基础。

Abstract: Recent demonstrations of quantum computational advantage have been driven largely by sampling problems. A prominent model, boson sampling, involves sampling from the output distribution of a linear optical network. However, its classical hardness hinges on two plausible yet less-studied conjectures: the average-case hardness of approximating Gaussian permanents, and the permanent anti-concentration conjecture (PACC). The PACC is a purely mathematical assertion regarding the distributional properties of random Gaussian matrices. While the typical magnitude of the permanent has been established for discrete random matrices, the complex Gaussian case, which governs transition amplitudes in linear optical networks, has remained open. Here, we establish a weak anti-concentration bound by upper-bounding the probability that a random Gaussian permanent is superexponentially smaller than its standard deviation. Tightening this bound to an inverse-polynomial fraction would prove the original PACC. As a corollary, we establish the typical magnitude of Gaussian permanents, on par with Tao and Vu's seminal result for Bernoulli matrices. Combined with the Aaronson-Arkhipov framework, our result implies that classically simulating boson sampling to within a superexponentially small total variation distance would collapse the polynomial hierarchy, assuming the remaining conjectures hold.

</details>


### [99] [Capacitive Loading in Two-dimensional Fluxonium Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22138)
*Quan Guan,Guo Xuan Chan,Xu Dou,Chunqing Deng,Lijing Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了电容加载与外部耦合的解析关系，发现约瑟夫森结及其阵列的寄生电容是二维磁通量子比特架构中电容加载的主要来源，而量子比特焊盘几何结构可缓解该问题。基于此提出设计原则，数值演示了超快、高保真度的两比特门，证明电容加载并非二维磁通量子处理器的根本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电容加载严重阻碍了磁通量子比特从一维向高度互联的二维架构扩展，但其物理起源尚不明确，亟需深入理解并找到解决方案。

Method: 推导量子比特电容预算与外部电容耦合之间的解析关系，识别主导电容加载的物理来源（约瑟夫森结及其阵列的寄生电容），并展示如何通过优化焊盘几何结构来缓解该问题。

Result: 发现约瑟夫森结和约瑟夫森结阵列的寄生电容是电容加载的主要来源，而量子比特焊盘几何结构可被工程化以缓解电容加载；数值实验表明可实现超快、高保真度的两比特门。

Conclusion: 电容加载并非二维磁通量子处理器的根本性限制，通过合理设计可以有效克服。

Abstract: Capacitive loading has emerged as a major obstacle to scaling fluxonium qubits from one-dimensional to highly connected two-dimensional (2D) architectures, yet its physical origin remains poorly understood. We derive an analytical relation between the qubit capacitance budget and the achievable capacitive coupling to external circuit elements, identifying the parasitic capacitances of Josephson junctions and Josephson junction arrays as the dominant source of capacitive loading while showing that the qubit-pad geometry can instead be engineered to mitigate it. Building on these insights, we formulate practical design principles and numerically demonstrate ultrafast, high-fidelity two-qubit gates in 2D fluxonium architectures. Our results reveal that capacitive loading does not constitute a fundamental limit for 2D fluxonium quantum processors.

</details>


### [100] [Molecular chiral discrimination through symmetry-breaking spin dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22154)
*Tianyu Xie,Yucheng Hao,Mingzhe Liu,Mengqi Wang,Shaoyi Xu,Zhiyuan Zhao,Chang-Kui Duan,Ya Wang,Fazhan Shi,Jiangfeng Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于磁共振（MR）的直接方法，通过构建附近核自旋的对称性破缺动力学，在单分子水平上确定分子手性，解决了传统MR对手性不敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性在多个科学领域至关重要，但现有单分子水平的手性区分和调控手段不足；传统磁共振方法由于系综平均丢失信息，且缺少手性试剂时无法区分分子手性，亟需新的方法突破。

Method: 通过构建附近核自旋的对称性破缺动力学，利用两种对映体在实空间中的镜像不对称性在自旋动力学中打破镜像反射与时间反演的联合对称性；实验上使用强耦合但未极化核自旋时两种对映体不可区分，自旋极化后打破场反转对称性的动力学结果使两者明显分化。

Result: 实验证明，两种对映体在未极化核自旋动力学中不可区分，但自旋极化后的动力学结果显著分化，成功实现了单分子水平的手性区分。

Conclusion: 该方法为化学和生物学中手性诱导性质的研究提供了新的有力工具，有望揭示手性相关现象的起源。

Abstract: Molecular chirality plays a crucial role in physics, chemistry, life sciences and pharmacology. Nowadays, the chiral discrimination and control at the single-molecule level is urgently needed to reveal the origin of the chirality-relevant phenomena by recovering the information disturbed by the ensemble averaging. The method of magnetic resonance (MR), as one of powerful tools for structure analysis, is blind to the molecular chirality in the absence of a chiral reagent. Here we propose and experimentally demonstrate a direct MR-based method for determining the chirality at the single-molecule level through constructing the symmetry-breaking dynamics of nearby nuclear spins. In principle, the mirror asymmetry of two enantiomers in real space is manifested by breaking the joint symmetry of the mirror reflection and time reversal in spin space under spin dynamics. Experimentally, two enantiomers are indistinguishable from the dynamics of strongly-coupled but unpolarized nuclear spins, but diverge evidently in the dynamical results that break the field-inversion symmetry after spins are polarized. Our method and results will benefit the study of chirality-induced properties in the fields of chemistry and biology.

</details>


### [101] [Maximal Fisher Budget Forces Blind Directions in Bipartite Collective SU(d) Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22195)
*Tariq Aziz,Naeem Akhtar,Dong Wang,Augusto Smerzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 纯两量子比特探针在单固定设置下估计SU(d)变换时，最大总Fisher灵敏度和完全局部可辨识性互斥。探针的交换对称性和集体极化决定了量子Fisher信息矩阵的迹。最大化解是交换对称且最大纠缠的，其Fisher矩阵在所有可见方向具有相等灵敏度，而d(d-1)/2个生成元方向不可见。弱兼容性迫使纯双粒子探针至少有⌊d/2⌋个盲方向。最大纠缠时探针定义的反酉不变量分类完整Fisher谱，且保持交换对称性的最优近似具有发散Holevo代价。对于N≥3，广义GHZ探针可同时满足弱兼容性、达到N体Fisher迹界并保持完全局部可辨识性。Fisher灵敏度、弱兼容性、可辨识性和可达精度是非对易生成元量子计量学中的不同资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示纯两量子比特探针在估计SU(d)变换时，最大Fisher灵敏度与完全局部可辨识性之间的互斥关系，并探究量子计量学中不同资源之间的根本性权衡。

Method: 通过推导量子Fisher信息矩阵迹与探针交换对称性和集体极化之间的精确恒等式，分析最大纠缠纯探针的Fisher谱结构，引入反酉不变量进行分类，并推广到多体情形。

Result: 最大灵敏度解必为交换对称且最大纠缠，Fisher矩阵在所有可见方向等灵敏度但d(d-1)/2个方向完全盲；弱兼容性迫使至少⌊d/2⌋个盲方向；反酉不变量分类完整Fisher谱；N≥3时广义GHZ态可突破双体限制。

Conclusion: Fisher灵敏度、弱兼容性、可辨识性和可达精度在非对易生成元量子计量学中是四种不同的资源，不可同时优化，需根据具体任务权衡选择。

Abstract: For pure two-qudit probes used in a single fixed setting to estimate a common SU(d) transformation, maximal total Fisher sensitivity and full local identifiability are mutually exclusive. We derive exact identities that determine the trace of the quantum Fisher information matrix from two measurable probe properties: exchange symmetry and collective polarization. Every maximizer is exchange symmetric and maximally entangled. Its Fisher matrix has equal sensitivity in every visible direction, while $d(d-1)/2$ generator directions are blind. More generally, weak compatibility, defined by vanishing mean generator commutators, forces at least $\lfloor d/2\rfloor$ blind directions for every pure bipartite probe. A probe-defined antiunitary invariant classifies the complete Fisher spectrum at maximal entanglement, and approaches to the optimum that preserve exchange symmetry have a divergent Holevo cost. The bipartite obstruction is sharp in particle number: for $N\geq 3$, generalized GHZ probes can satisfy weak compatibility, attain the corresponding $N$-partite Fisher trace bound, and retain full local identifiability. Hence, Fisher sensitivity, weak compatibility, identifiability, and attainable precision are distinct resources in quantum metrology with noncommuting generators.

</details>


### [102] [Dark Polaron Theory for High Intensity Laser Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22243)
*Marcel Morillas-Rozas,Alberto López-García,Enamul Haque,Gonzalo Reina Rivero,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"暗极化子"概念，解决了传统离子捕获激光冷却方案受限于弱激光强度和小的Lamb-Dicke参数的问题，揭示了高激光强度下电磁诱导透明冷却速率受限的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离子捕获激光冷却和门控方案受限于弱激光强度和小的Lamb-Dicke参数，需要克服这一限制以扩展激光控制方案的应用范围。

Method: 提出了暗极化子概念——一种与lambda激光配置完全解耦的扩展赝自旋极化态。该方法将所有高阶Lamb-Dicke项坍缩为与激光强度无关的单一线性耦合。

Result: 应用该理论成功阐明了近期高强度激光电磁诱导透明实验实现中观察到的冷却速率限制的原因。

Conclusion: 暗极化子概念为理解高激光强度下离子冷却机制提供了新的理论框架，突破了传统弱场近似理论的解释局限。

Abstract: Conventional laser control schemes for cooling and gate operation of trapped ions are limited to the regime of weak laser intensities and small Lamb-Dicke parameters. To overcome this limitation, we present the concept of dark polarons: spatially extended states of pseudospin polarization that are fully decoupled from a lambda laser configuration. In this picture, all high-order Lamb-Dicke terms collapse into a single linear coupling independent of laser intensity. We apply it to definitively elucidate the reasons behind cooling rate limitations observed in recent experimental implementations of electromagnetically induced transparency with high-intensity lasers.

</details>


### [103] [Lower bounds for the CNOT-complexity of linear reversible operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22248)
*Søren Fuglede Jørgensen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了可逆矩阵在F₂上的CNOT复杂度，提出了一种从纠错码校验矩阵构造显式矩阵族的方法，其CNOT复杂度至少为4n - o(n)，渐近地超过了循环置换的3(n-1)，并给出了一个n=17167的显式矩阵实例，其复杂度超过了同维度的循环置换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知最大CNOT复杂度为Θ(n²/log n)，但此前缺乏一个显式矩阵族能达到超线性复杂度，最难的显式族仅是循环置换的3(n-1)。本文旨在构造具有更高CNOT复杂度的显式矩阵族。

Method: 将不可逆线性算子的加法复杂度下界以较小的损失提升到可逆设定中，利用纠错码的校验矩阵来构造显式矩阵族，并分析其CNOT复杂度。

Result: 构造的显式矩阵族CNOT复杂度至少为4n - o(n)，渐近超越循环置换；并给出了一个具体的n=17167的显式矩阵，其CNOT复杂度超过了同维度循环置换。

Conclusion: 通过将加法复杂度下界提升到可逆设定，利用纠错码构造的显式矩阵族在CNOT复杂度上取得了渐近改进，并提供了具体的超越实例。

Abstract: The CNOT-complexity of an invertible matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$ is the minimum number of CNOT gates needed to synthesize the corresponding linear reversible operator. While the maximum CNOT-complexity over all $n \times n$ matrices is known to be $Θ(n^2 / \log n)$, no explicit family of matrices requiring a superlinear number of CNOT gates is known, and until now the hardest explicitly known family has been the cyclic permutations, with CNOT-complexity $3(n-1)$. We show that lower bounds for the additive complexity of not-necessarily-reversible linear operators can be lifted to the reversible setting with only a small loss. As an application, we use this to describe an explicit family of matrices, constructed from parity-check matrices of error-correcting codes, with CNOT-complexity at least $4n - o(n)$, asymptotically surpassing the cyclic permutations. Moreover, this construction yields an explicit matrix $A \in \mathrm{GL}_{n}(\mathbb{F}_2)$, $n = 17167$, whose CNOT-complexity exceeds that of the cyclic permutation on $n$ symbols.

</details>


### [104] [Low loss superconducting resonators enabled by aluminum microstructural engineering and dielectric trimming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22265)
*Mahmoud Almansouri,Umar T. F. Alhuwaymel,Ibraheem AlYousef,Albaraa Shafi,Abdullah Albogime,Igor Getmanov,Ahmed Hajr,Atif Shamim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导电路中铝膜晶粒尺寸与介电边缘粗糙度分别对非TLS和TLS损耗的影响，通过加热沉积优化铝微结构增大晶粒尺寸、提出Tropic两步刻蚀技术获得超光滑硅表面，将谐振器品质因子从6×10⁴提升至2.3×10⁶（提高两个数量级），为超导量子器件的性能提升提供了制造路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中的材料损耗从根本上限制了量子比特相干时间和谐振器品质因子，而超导金属的本征特性及介电边缘平滑度与TLS和非TLS损耗之间的关联尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1) 通过沉积过程中加热来调控铝膜的微结构，增大晶粒尺寸、降低晶界密度，以减少金属相关的非TLS损耗；2) 提出两步刻蚀技术（Tropic etching），制备超光滑硅表面以抑制介电TLS损耗。

Result: 品质因子从6×10⁴提升至2.3×10⁶，实现了两个数量级的改善。

Conclusion: 铝微结构的优化和边缘刻蚀技术的改进可显著提升超导量子器件的性能，且由于铝是大多数高相干约瑟夫森结的基础材料，介电边缘普遍存在于常见器件几何结构中，因此该研究具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: Material losses in superconducting circuits fundamentally limit qubit coherence times and resonator quality factors. Most research efforts focus on mitigating losses at circuit interfaces, including metal--substrate, substrate--air, and metal--air interfaces. However, the correlation between TLS and non-TLS losses with the intrinsic properties of the superconducting metal and the dielectric edge smoothness is not well studied. In this work, we link the aluminum film grain size to non-TLS losses and the dielectric trimming profile and roughness to TLS loss; both loss mechanisms are subsequently mitigated. To reduce metal-related losses, we engineer the aluminum microstructure by heating during deposition, increasing grain size and reducing grain boundary density. Beyond mitigating metal losses, we introduce a two-step etching technique, Tropic etching, to suppress dielectric TLS loss by producing an ultra-smooth silicon surface with minimal defects and redeposition. These results lay out the fabrication pathway for aluminum resonators with lower loss, demonstrating two-orders-of-magnitude improvement in quality factor from $6\times10^{4}$ to $2.3\times10^{6}$. Since aluminum is the basis for most high-coherence Josephson junctions and dielectric edges are inherent to all common device geometries, the improvements in aluminum microstructure and edge profiling, presented here, can enhance the performance of superconducting quantum devices.

</details>


### [105] [The Threshold Theorem in Watts: Fault Tolerance as a Question About Objective Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22276)
*Amit Hagar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Hagar & Sergioli提出的资源受限客观概率解释应用于容错量子计算（FTQC），指出阈值定理本质上是关于状态可分类的论断。作者发现原始推导忽略了四项关键资源成本（设备校准、解码、相干性、熵清除），并提出以"瓦特每十倍的逻辑错误抑制"作为FTQC可行性的可测量指标，同时指出已有文献中已包含该指标的首批数据点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为容错量子计算的可行性争议提供客观概率解释框架，将原本基于噪声模型假设的讨论转化为关于资源受限客观概率的定量争议，从而为实证解决该争论提供可测量的指标。

Method: 应用Hagar & Sergioli的资源受限客观概率解释到FTQC场景，识别出原始阈值定理推导中遗漏的四项资源成本（设备校准、解码、相干性、熵清除），并提出"瓦特每十倍的逻辑错误抑制"作为量化FTQC可行性的指标。

Result: 指出已有文献已包含该新指标的首批数据点，并提出了两个能够经验性地解决FTQC争议的关键测量。

Conclusion: 持续了三年的FTQC辩论，在资源受限概率解释下，本质上是对目标逻辑态资源受限客观概率的定量争议，而非单纯的噪声模型假设分歧。

Abstract: In 2011 Hagar & Sergioli proposed a new interpretation of objective probability in deterministic physics. On it, the probability of a physical state supervenes on the resources, energy over time, required to realize it from a given state, relative to the resources available (arXiv:1101.3521). The motivation for that paper was an objective alternative to QBism, the view that sees quantum probabilities as subjective degrees of belief. Here I apply this interpretation to a more practical subject matter: fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). Under the resource-bounded measure Hagar & Sergioli proposed, the threshold theorem becomes a claim about classification: it asserts that error-corrected logical states belong to the class of relatively cheaply realizable states, whose probability remains near 1 as the machine grows. The theorem originally derived this claim from a resource inventory that was partial, and left out four resources consumed by error correction: calibration of a drifting device, decoding within the correction cycle, coherence as a finite time budget, and entropy flush through fresh ancillas. These entered the original derivation at zero price. Here I translate the feasibility of FTQC into a measurable quantity, watts per decade of suppressed logical error (a decade, in the engineer's usage, being one factor of ten in the error rate). I then show that the published record already contains its first data points for this translation, and I state the two measurements that would settle the question empirically. The three-decade debate on FTQC, conducted so far as an exchange about noise-model assumptions, turns out to be, under this interpretation, a quantitative dispute about a single object: the resource-bounded objective probability of the target logical states.

</details>


### [106] [Klein tunneling through an asymmetric barrier: Symmetric transmission and directional pair creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22337)
*Andre G. Campos,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单通道渐近导线下，克莱因隧穿通过空间不对称势垒时左右入射的透射概率相同。通过时变Wigner函数模拟发现，方向性缺失存在于势垒内部：尖锐边缘产生的亚势垒负能态粒子数比平滑边缘多出数倍。因此克莱因隧穿中的方向性控制源于粒子对产生而非透射电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究克莱因隧穿中空间不对称势垒是否会导致左右入射方向上的透射概率不对称性，并寻找方向性控制的实际机制。

Method: 理论证明与数值模拟相结合：首先从理论上证明单通道情形下透射概率的对称性；然后使用时变Wigner函数数值模拟来观察势垒内部动力学过程。

Result: 左右入射的透射概率完全对称；方向性差异出现在势垒内部，尖锐边缘产生的亚势垒负能态粒子数远多于平滑边缘。

Conclusion: 克莱因隧穿中的方向性控制机制不是透射电流，而是势垒边缘形状决定的粒子对产生过程。

Abstract: We prove that the transmission probability for the Klein tunneling through a spatially asymmetric barrier is the same for left and right incidence whenever each asymptotic lead carries a single propagating channel per direction. Time-dependent Wigner-function simulations confirm this and locate the missing directionality in the barrier's interior, where a sharp edge generates several times more under-barrier negative-energy population than a smooth one. Directional control in the Klein regime therefore resides in pair production rather than in the transmitted current.

</details>


### [107] [Metrology of quantum imaging schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22373)
*Emma Brambila,Giacomo Sorelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子多参数估计框架，系统比较了基于空间关联光子对的量子成像方案（鬼成像、双光子成像、非探测光子成像），计算了量子Fisher信息矩阵，发现鬼成像和双光子成像在透射估计精度上优于非探测光子成像，但后者在避免不同空间模式间的透射估计耦合方面具有天然优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子成像方案缺乏统一的性能比较框架，不同方案在透射估计精度和模式间耦合特性上的优劣尚不明确，需要从量子多参数估计角度进行系统评估。

Method: 将量子成像方案形式化为量子多参数估计问题，以不同空间模式的透射系数为待估参数；计算各方案的量子Fisher信息矩阵，并证明它们可由物体模式基下的测量饱和，该测量概括了各成像配置中的常见检测方案。

Result: 鬼成像和双光子成像在透射估计精度上普遍优于非探测光子成像；非探测光子成像是唯一天然避免不同空间模式间透射估计耦合的方案。

Conclusion: 不同量子成像方案各有优势：鬼成像和双光子成像适合高精度透射估计任务，非探测光子成像适合需要避免模式间耦合的场景。结果为基于空间关联的量子传感技术设计和优化提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We compare the performance of quantum imaging schemes based on spatially correlated photon pairs by formulating them as quantum multiparameter estimation problems, in which the object is characterized by transmission coefficients associated with different spatial modes. Our work focuses on standard quantum imaging techniques such as ghost imaging, two-photon imaging, and imaging with undetected photons. Specifically, we compute the quantum Fisher information matrices and show that they are saturated by Fisher information matrices corresponding to measurements in the object-mode basis, which generalize the common detection schemes employed in each imaging configuration. We find that ghost imaging and two-photon imaging generally provide higher precision for transmission estimation than imaging with undetected photons, but the latter is the only one that naturally does not couple transmission estimation across different spatial modes. These results identify which imaging protocols are best suited for specific tasks and provide practical guidelines for the design and optimization of quantum sensing technologies based on spatial correlations.

</details>


### [108] [Explicit block-encodings for biharmonic boundary-value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22396)
*Chuwen Ma,Zihao Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对双调和方程的高维离散化导致的自由度快速增长和严重病态问题，发展了基于QSVT-VTAA的量子线性系统算法。针对周期、简单支撑和Dirichlet-Neumann三种边界条件，构建了显式块编码：周期和简单支撑问题通过Fourier和正弦变换对角化得到条件数与二阶算子相当的增广Poisson系统；Dirichlet-Neumann问题则引入了二阶边界校正有限差分离散化，建立了与网格无关的稳定性，并构造了非对称矩阵的显式块编码。还提出了带额外边界未知量的耦合Laplace系统，分析了其复杂度。数值实验验证了所提离散化和线性求解的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和方程是高维四阶偏微分方程，其离散化面临自由度快速增长和严重病态两大挑战。传统方法难以高效求解，而量子算法有潜力突破维数灾难和条件数瓶颈，因此需要为不同边界条件设计适合量子实现的块编码和线性系统求解方案。

Method: 采用QSVT-VTAA量子线性系统算法框架，针对三种边界条件分别构造：(1) 周期边界：基于Fourier变换对角化；(2) 简单支撑边界：基于正弦变换对角化，两者均转化为条件数更优的增广Poisson系统；(3) Dirichlet-Neumann边界：引入二阶边界校正有限差分离散化，证明网格无关稳定性，构造非对称矩阵的显式块编码；并进一步提出耦合Laplace系统处理额外边界未知量。分析涵盖离散误差、块编码归一化、门复杂度和解提取。

Result: 三种边界条件下的离散化均成功构造了适用于QSVT-VTAA算法的显式块编码。周期和简单支撑问题的条件数降低至二阶算子水平；Dirichlet-Neumann问题实现了网格无关稳定性。数值实验验证了所提离散化方案和量子线性求解的正确性与有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为双调和方程的高效量子求解提供了系统化的显式块编码构造方法，覆盖了三种典型边界条件，并给出了完整的收敛性和复杂度分析，为四阶PDE的量子算法设计奠定了理论和实现基础。

Abstract: The biharmonic equation is a prototypical fourth-order partial differential equation whose high-dimensional discretization suffers from rapidly growing degrees of freedom and severe ill-conditioning. We develop QSVT--VTAA quantum linear-system algorithms by constructing explicit block-encodings tailored to periodic, simply supported, and Dirichlet--Neumann boundary conditions. For periodic and simply supported problems, Fourier and sine-transform diagonalizations yield augmented Poisson systems with the condition-number scaling of a second-order operator. For Dirichlet--Neumann problems, we introduce a second-order boundary-corrected finite-difference discretization, establish mesh-independent stability, and construct an explicit block-encoding of the resulting nonsymmetric matrix. We also formulate a coupled-Laplace system with additional boundary unknowns and characterize its complexity in terms of the condition number of the complete augmented matrix. The analysis covers discretization error, block-encoding normalization, gate complexity, and solution extraction under an amplitude-input and quantum-state-output model. Numerical experiments validate the proposed discretizations and the corresponding linear solves.

</details>


### [109] [A route to damage tolerance exceeding $10\%$ in shuttling-equipped quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22429)
*Quinten Eggerickx,Simon C. Benjamin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种针对固态容错量子计算中缺陷（丢失）的高容错方法，基于CAbLECAR方法，通过ancilla沿预编程路径移动来执行表面码的stabiliser测量。在物理晶格受损时，利用路径规划软件找到满足stabiliser对易和hook错误避免约束的高效路径，并转换为Stim探测器错误模型计算逻辑错误率。结果表明，在10%损伤下，等效表面码距离约为完整晶格的48%（大阵列极限），小阵列可提升至60%，表明可通过构建过大规模阵列来容忍显著损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子计算中物理缺陷（如制造过程中的丢失或运行时出现的错误）会严重降低容错量子纠错码的性能，亟需一种能够高容错地应对这些缺陷的方法。

Method: 基于CAbLECAR方法，ancilla沿预编程路径移动以执行stabiliser测量；在受损物理晶格上，利用路径规划软件在stabiliser对易和hook错误避免约束下寻找高效路径；将解转换为Stim探测器错误模型以计算逻辑错误率。

Result: 在合理错误率下，10%损伤导致等效表面码距离约为完整晶格的48%（大阵列极限），小阵列下可升至约60%。

Conclusion: 通过构建过大规模阵列可容忍显著损伤，且该方法可推广至其他qLDPC码，并潜在适应运行时出现的损伤。

Abstract: This is a short study of an approach offering high tolerance to damage (i.e. defects or 'drop outs') in solid state fault-tolerant quantum computing. Our method is primarily aimed at semiconductor electron spin-qubit systems, which have been shown to support fast and high-fidelity shuttling along pre-defined paths. We adapt the recent CAbLECAR method of Chadwick and Chong: stabilisers are performed by ancillas which each follow a bespoke pre-programmed path. We consider the simple surface code but we damage the physical lattice, and rely on route-solving software to find efficient pathways under constraints enforcing stabiliser commutation and hook error avoidance. Solutions are then converted to detector error models for Stim and logical error rates are obtained. We express our results by gauging the logical performance against that of a pristine lattice, using the notion of a reduced equivalent surface-code distance; for reasonable underlying error rates we find that $10\%$ damage leaves roughly half of the pristine equivalent distance ($d_\text{equiv}\approx0.48\,d_\text{pristine}$ in the large-array limit, rising to $\approx0.60$ for our smallest array). This suggests that one can tolerate substantial damage by building oversized arrays. We note that investigating damage tolerance of other qLDPC codes is a straightforward generalisation, and potentially one could adapt to damage emerging at runtime.

</details>


### [110] [Highly indistinguishable photons from a tin-vacancy spin qubit in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22439)
*Dennis Herrmann,Robert Morsch-Golsong,Tobias Bauer,Marlon Schäfer,David Lindler,Linus Ehre,Peter van Loock,Matthew Markham,Nicola Palmer,Soumen Mandal,Oliver Williams,Christoph Becher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了金刚石中锡空位中心产生高不可区分性单光子（原始HOM干涉可见度>0.95，本征不可区分性达0.999），并通过量子频率转换至电信C波段保持其不可区分性，结合长寿命自旋相干性，证明该平台在量子中继器应用中超越直接传输极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要光子不可区分性以实现高保真度双光子干涉（如Bell态测量和融合门），但现有光子源在长距离量子中继应用中面临退相干和损耗挑战，亟需兼具高不可区分性、长自旋相干时间和电信波段兼容性的固态量子平台。

Method: 利用相干激发金刚石锡空位中心产生单光子；通过Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉测量原始可见度；分离本征发射器特性与技术缺陷以提取本征不可区分性；实施量子频率转换至电信C波段并验证不可区分性保持；结合Monte Carlo仿真评估量子中继链路性能。

Result: 原始HOM干涉可见度超过0.95；本征不可区分性高达0.999；退相干影响可忽略，剩余限制主要源于技术缺陷；频率转换后光子不可区分性得以保持；Monte Carlo仿真显示SnV中心平台超越直接传输的量子中继性能边界。

Conclusion: 锡空位中心在金刚石中是一种有竞争力的长距离量子网络和光子量子信息处理平台，尤其适用于量子中继器应用。

Abstract: Quantum networks promise secure communication, distributed sensing and modular quantum computing by interconnecting distant quantum nodes through photonic links. Extending such networks beyond metropolitan distances requires quantum repeaters to overcome the exponential attenuation of photons in optical fiber. Across all architectures, a key requirement is the indistinguishability of single photons, which directly impacts the fidelity of photonic operations based on two-photon interference, such as Bell-state measurements and fusion gates. Here, we demonstrate generation of highly indistinguishable single photons from a coherently excited tin-vacancy center in diamond, achieving raw Hong-Ou-Mandel interference visibilities exceeding 0.95. By separating intrinsic emitter properties from technical imperfections, we show that decoherence plays a negligible role and that the remaining limitations are predominantly technical in nature, arriving at an intrinsic indistinguishability of up to 0.999. We further show that quantum frequency conversion to the telecom C-band preserves the photon indistinguishability. In combination with the long-lived electron and nuclear spin coherence times, these results establish tin-vacancy centers in diamond as a competitive platform for long-distance quantum networks and photonic quantum information processing. We further substantiate this potential through Monte Carlo simulations of a quantum-repeater link, demonstrating that the SnV-center platform surpasses the bound set by direct transmission.

</details>


### [111] [Algebraic structure of Tiger codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22460)
*Clément Poirson,Anthony Leverrier,Christophe Vuillot*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Tiger码这一多模玻色子量子码族进行了严格的代数处理：证明了湮灭型约束的有限生成性，构造了显式正交基，揭示了其链复形同调逻辑结构，建立了码空间上的傅里叶变换以给出X/Z型对偶描述，推广至非线性数约束，并系统研究了逻辑操作（包括Pauli算符推广与非Clifford门构造），为这类玻色子编码提供了数学严格的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tiger码统一了猫码、配对猫码、双模二项式码等多种玻色子量子码，但缺乏严格的代数理论基础。本文旨在提供严格的数学处理，证明其核心性质，并扩展至更广泛的码族和逻辑操作实现。

Method: 从核定义出发证明湮灭型约束的有限生成集存在性；构造显式标准正交基；利用链复形同调分析逻辑结构；发展码空间上的傅里叶变换以证明相位旋转投影相干态的稠密性并导出X/Z对偶描述；扩展至非线性数约束（如四腿猫码、重复猫码）；推广逻辑Pauli算符并利用物理多项式相位旋转构造非Clifford门，通过单变量多项式分解刻画保持码空间的相位旋转多项式及其Clifford层次。

Result: 证明了湮灭约束的有限生成性；构造了显式正交基；建立了同调逻辑结构；证明了相位旋转投影相干态在码空间中稠密；得到了X/Z对偶描述；将框架推广至非线性约束；推广了Pauli算符构造；给出了多项式相位旋转保持码空间的判据，并分解为单变量多项式族以关联Clifford层次。

Conclusion: Tiger码是一个数学上严格的统一框架，适用于描述广泛的玻色子编码，其逻辑操作（包括非Clifford门）可以通过物理多项式相位旋转系统实现。

Abstract: Tiger codes form a family of multimode bosonic quantum codes that unify several previously known constructions, including cat, paircat, and the two-mode binomial code. In this work, we give a rigorous algebraic treatment of these codes. Starting from a kernel definition of the codespace, we prove that the annihilation-type constraints admit a finite generating set, construct an explicit orthonormal basis, and show that the logical structure of the code is governed by the homology of an underlying chain complex, as expected in the original work on Tiger codes of Xu et al. We then develop a Fourier transform over the codespace to prove that the span of phase-rotated projected coherent states is dense therein, and to yield dual $X$- and $Z$-type descriptions of the code. We further extend the framework to non-linear number constraints, encompassing codes such as the four-legged cat or the repetition cat code. Finally, we investigate the implementation of logical operations. We first generalise the construction of logical Pauli operators proposed by Xu et al. to arbitrary logical spaces, and then construct non-Clifford gates using physical polynomial phase rotations of the form $e^{iP(\hat{\boldsymbol{n}})}$. We derive criteria on the real polynomial $P$ which, for positive single-logical-qubit Tiger codes satisfying an additional sign assumption, such as the paircat code, characterise the polynomials $P$ that preserve the codespace by decomposing them into a family of univariate polynomials. Through this decomposition, we relate the degrees of the resulting components to the induced logical action in the Clifford hierarchy. These results establish Tiger codes as a mathematically robust framework for describing a broad class of bosonic encodings.

</details>


### [112] [Multiplexed storage and interaction of Rydberg spinwaves via the gradient echo memory protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22466)
*Bartosz Niewelt,Stanisław Kurzyna,Bartosz Kasza,Wojciech Wasilewski,Michał Parniak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新式多光子寻址方案（Ń型能级结构），通过近零动量转移的集体里德伯激发，将里德伯自旋波寿命延长近十倍，并兼容梯度回波记忆（GEM）协议，实现了具有可控长程相互作用的多模量子存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体里德伯激发虽具有强可控相互作用，但在与梯度回波记忆（GEM）等时间/频谱复用方案集成时，因大自旋波波矢导致的快速运动退相干问题严重受限。

Method: 设计Ń型能级结构，利用两束额外的非共振驱动场以魔角排列形成闭合波矢环路，实现近零动量转移的集体里德伯激发；同时保持与GEM诱导的非均匀展宽兼容，并通过相邻里德伯态间的微波耦合控制存储模间的衰减与衍射。

Result: 里德伯自旋波寿命延长近十倍；成功实现长寿命里德伯自旋波在多模量子记忆中的存储与操控；演示了通过相互作用诱导的衰减控制存储模间的衰减，以及检索光信号的相互作用控制衍射。

Conclusion: 该工作重建了里德伯激发与GEM协议之间的兼容性，为具有可控长程相互作用的多模量子存储器提供了可行路径，可应用于量子网络、量子传感和量子信息处理。

Abstract: Collective Rydberg excitations offer strong and controllable interactions for quantum information processing, sensing, and nonlinear quantum optics, but their integration with temporally or spectrally multiplexed schemes, such as the Gradient Echo Memory (GEM) protocol, is hindered by rapid motional dephasing caused by the large spinwave wavevector. We demonstrate a new type of multi-photon addressing and interfacing scheme (with levels following the shape of the letter Ń) that allows us to generate collective Rydberg excitations with near-zero momentum transfer, extending the Rydberg spinwave lifetime almost tenfold. The scheme relies on two additional off-resonant driving fields arranged at a magic angle, forming a closed wavevector loop while remaining compatible with GEM-induced inhomogeneous broadening. This enables storage and manipulation of long-lived Rydberg spinwaves in a multimode quantum memory. Using microwave coupling between neighboring Rydberg states, we can control the attenuation between stored excitation modes by interaction-induced decay and demonstrate interaction-controlled diffraction of a retrieved optical signal. Our results reestablish compatibility between Rydberg excitations and GEM, providing a route toward multimode quantum memories with controllable long-range interactions and applications in quantum networking, sensing, and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [113] [Learning to Prepare Molecular Ground States with Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22468)
*Alex Koziell-Pipe,Jasmine Brewer,Jem Guhit,Marwa H. Farag,Kripa Panchagnula,Gabriel Laude,Fabian Finger,Carlo Gaggioli,Ludmila Szulakowska,Oliver J. Backhouse,Christos Papalitsas,Jason G. Mustakis,Thomas Soini,David Munoz Ramo,Stephen Clark,Elica Kyoseva,Enrico Rinaldi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT-GQE，一种生成式AI框架，通过学习合成电子结构计算的基态制备电路。该方法利用ADAPT-VQE生成高质量参考电路训练模型，再通过强化学习提升电路生成精度，在保持或改善状态制备精度的同时实现数量级的电路生成时间缩减，并在Quantinuum Helios-1量子硬件上成功演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备是许多量子算法的关键组件，但现有迭代算法（如ADAPT-VQE）对于材料科学和制药开发中涉及的大分子计算成本过高，需要更高效的电路合成方法。

Method: 首先使用ADAPT-VQE生成高质量参考电路作为训练目标；然后训练生成模型学习电路合成；最后利用强化学习对模型提出的电路进行评分和优化，使电路生成精度超越训练数据。

Result: 与ADAPT-VQE相比，电路生成时间实现数量级缩减，同时保持或改善状态制备精度；在Quantinuum Helios-1量子硬件上成功运行生成的电路，实现了AI生成量子化学电路在先进量子硬件上的里程碑。

Conclusion: 该方法为实用规模的量子计算化学自动化量子电路合成建立了可行路径，展示了生成式AI在量子计算中解决大规模分子问题的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a key component of many quantum algorithms. Performing this step efficiently is essential for realizing practical quantum advantage in quantum chemistry applications. Iterative algorithms like ADAPT-VQE can produce shallow ground-state preparation circuits, but become computationally prohibitive for the larger molecules relevant to materials science and pharmaceutical development. Here, we introduce ADAPT-GQE, a generative AI framework that learns to synthesize ground-state preparation circuits for electronic structure calculations. We first use ADAPT-VQE to generate high-quality reference circuits, which are then used as targets for training models for circuit generation. Once trained, the model can efficiently propose and score circuits, enabling reinforcement learning (RL) to drive circuit generation accuracy beyond the accuracy of the ADAPT-VQE training data. This pipeline achieves order-of-magnitude reductions in circuit generation time relative to ADAPT-VQE while maintaining comparable or improved state-preparation accuracy. We demonstrate ADAPT-GQE on imipramine, a well-established tricyclic antidepressant that serves as a representative, challenging target for computational modelling in drug stability protocols. We execute generated circuits on Quantinuum Helios-1, representing a milestone for AI-generated quantum chemistry circuits on state-of-the-art quantum hardware. These results establish a pathway toward automated quantum circuit synthesis for utility-scale quantum computational chemistry.

</details>


### [114] [Automated Flag-based Fault-Tolerant State Preparation using Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22498)
*Ben Criger,Aaron Hankin,Justin J. Burau,Abigail R. Perry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于电路规范算子（circuit gauge operator）形式的整数线性规划方法，用于自动优化容错稳定子态制备电路。该方法在[[7,1,3]]Steane码和[[24,10,4]]双块群代数码上实现了低于现有最优方法的门数，能检测最多三个错误，并在Quantinuum H2量子计算机上以10,000次实验验证了低逻辑块错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算中，后选择稳定子态制备是关键子程序，但现有方法（SAT-based和flag-at-origin）对于大码率自动优化效果有限。手工寻找低门数电路对大码不可行，因此需要自动化方法以降低门数、减少时间并提高可靠性。

Method: 利用电路规范算子（circuit gauge operator）形式化表达flag电路的构造，将其转化为整数线性规划（ILP）问题，从而自动搜索容错稳定子态制备电路，使门数低于或等于现有最优方法，并能检测最多三个错误。

Result: 推导出[[24,10,4]]双块群代数码的Steane纠错gadget，在Quantinuum System Model H2量子计算机上10,000次实验中，逻辑块错误率约0.00014（每逻辑量子比特约0.000014），约1.6%的shots因权重二错误被后选择。

Conclusion: 提出的ILP方法能自动构造门数更低的容错稳定子态制备电路，并在实际量子硬件上验证了其有效性，为大码的容错量子计算提供了实用化工具。

Abstract: Post-selected stabilizer state preparation is a necessary subroutine in fault-tolerant quantum computation, both for initialization of logical qubits, and for logical-ancilla-based error correction gadgets (e.g. Steane and Knill). Therefore, reducing the number of gates needed to prepare a stabilizer state fault-tolerantly can simultaneously reduce time-to-solution and increase reliability. For small, low-distance codes such as the [[7, 1, 3]] Steane code, circuits with low gate counts can be found by inspection. This becomes impractical for larger codes, necessitating automation. There are two state-of-the-art methods for automated fault-tolerant state preparation, SAT-based stabilizer measurement and flag-at-origin. In this work, we optimize state preparation circuits using the circuit gauge operator formalism to express the construction of flag circuits as an integer linear program. This allows the construction of circuits with equal or lower gate count than the state of the art, while detecting up to three errors. We use this technique to derive a Steane error correction gadget for the [[24, 10, 4]] two-block group algebra code, and test it on Quantinuum's System Model H2 quantum computer with 10,000 shots, resulting in a logical block error rate ~0.00014 (~0.000014 per logical qubit), with ~1.6% of the shots post-selected due to weight-two errors.

</details>


### [115] [Correlated Coherent Errors in Stabilizer Codes: A General Cumulant Framework and Interference-Based Error Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22503)
*Rohan N Rajmohan,Antoine Brillant,Peter Groszkowski,Alireza Seif,Jens Koch,Aashish Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了稳定子码在相关相干Z噪声下经过多次QEC循环后的精确逻辑信道，并发展了一种累积量展开框架，得到噪声平均逻辑保真度的非微扰解析表达式。该框架适用于任意稳定子码和任意相关结构。关键发现是所诱导的逻辑信道依赖于代码空间的选择（即在标准随机Pauli错误模型中不存在这一特性），据此提出PROSE编码策略，通过选择最优稳定子本征空间来压制错误。该策略结合逻辑Pauli twirling可匹配或优于标准错误压制技术。此外，噪声相关性可被转化为资源：在正确编码下，正相关性反而能降低逻辑保真度低于无相关基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子码中相干错误在量子比特和QEC周期间常存在相关性，缺乏通用的解析处理方法。现有随机Pauli错误模型无法捕捉相干错误的特征，亟需一种一般性理论框架来理解相关相干噪声对QEC的影响。

Method: (1) 精确推导了重复QEC循环下相关相干Z噪声诱导的逻辑信道；(2) 发展累积量展开（cumulant-expansion）框架，得到噪声平均逻辑保真度的非微扰解析表达式；(3) 基于该表达式揭示逻辑信道对稳定子本征空间选择的依赖性，提出PROSE（protected stabilizer eigenspace）编码策略；(4) 结合逻辑Pauli twirling进行性能对比。

Result: (1) 逻辑信道依赖于稳定子本征空间选择，这是随机Pauli模型不存在的特征；(2) PROSE编码可有效识别并选择最优代码空间；(3) 结合逻辑Pauli twirling后，PROSE匹配或优于动态去耦和物理量子比特Pauli twirling等标准技术；(4) 噪声正相关性在某些编码下反而能降低逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 相关相干噪声不应被视为纯粹的危害；通过正确的编码策略（如PROSE），噪声相关性可转化为资源。该工作提供了一个广泛适用的、非微扰的分析框架，为理解和压制稳定子码中的相干相关噪声提供了新视角。

Abstract: Coherent errors in stabilizer codes are often correlated across qubits and QEC cycles. Having a general analytical treatment of such noise would thus be extremely valuable. We derive here the exact logical channel induced by repeated QEC cycles under correlated coherent $Z$ noise, and develop a broadly general cumulant-expansion framework that yields a tractable expression for the noise-averaged logical infidelity. Crucially, this expression is non-perturbative in the noise, and applies to arbitrary stabilizer codes and correlation structures. It reveals a feature with no analogue in standard stochastic Pauli error models: the induced channel depends on which stabilizer eigenspace is chosen as the codespace. Exploiting this, we introduce protected stabilizer eigenspace (PROSE) encoding, an error-suppression strategy that selects the optimal codespace. We show that this eigenspace can be efficiently identified in many relevant situations. Further, when combined with logical Pauli twirling, PROSE matches or outperforms standard error suppression techniques (dynamical decoupling, Pauli twirling of physical qubits). We also show that noise correlations, usually assumed to be harmful to QEC, can instead be a resource: with the right encoding, even positive correlations reduce the logical infidelity below the uncorrelated baseline. Our results offer a new, broadly applicable lens on correlated coherent noise in stabilizer codes.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Spectral Model: Data Reuploading with Input-Conditioned Frequency Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22516)
*Peiyong Wang,Udaya Parampalli,Casey R. Myers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A central design principle in modern machine learning and artificial intelligence is to align a model's inductive bias with the structure of its input data. For matrix-valued inputs, relevant matrix-level relationships can be characterised through spectral values and spectral subspaces; however, common coordinate-wise rotation-gate data-encoding unitaries used in most quantum machine learning models do not explicitly construct such a matrix-level representation. We introduce Quantum Spectral Models (QSMs), in which we construct the generator of the data-encoding unitary directly from each input matrix. We study three QSM variants based on symmetric, global block, and non-overlapping patch-local block Hamiltonians. Their outputs admit truncated Fourier representations in which input-dependent spectral gaps supply candidate phase carriers, while spectral subspaces help determine their coefficients. We evaluate the QSMs and comparison quantum models on two matrix representations of Pendigits and two controlled synthetic tasks defined by spectral statistics. At the largest evaluated circuit depth, QSM variants lead the tested quantum models in mean test accuracy across all four benchmarks. The patch-local QSM leads on Pendigits, whereas the global block-Hamiltonian QSM leads on the controlled spectral tasks. Ablations show a task-dependent reversal: subspace-preserving controls perform better on Pendigits, whereas spectral-value-only controls lead among the tested ablations on the synthetic tasks. Together, these results shed new light on quantum machine-learning model design by showing how input-conditioned spectral representations can provide an analysable inductive bias, while offering a broader perspective on structure-aware model design in machine learning and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [117] [Critical Sensing with Autonomous Devices: The Self-Oscillation Threshold of a Frequency-Locked NV-Centre Magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22521)
*Joan Toledo Aguilera,Gonzalo Reina Rivero,Marcel Morillas-Rozas,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新型量子传感器工作模式：故意将反馈锁定的NV色心磁力计驱动至离散反馈映射的翻转（周期倍增）分岔点之外，使其成为自持振荡器。推导了临界增益条件Gc=2Dcal/Dtrue，建立了阈值与可测量标量之间的关系，并通过实验验证了分岔图和临界涨落行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精密量子传感器以反馈锁定至自旋共振为标准工作模式。本文试图突破这一范式，通过将锁定系统故意驱动到其稳定区域之外（分岔点之外），探索传感器的新物理行为与工作机制，揭示反馈回路自激振荡的物理本质。

Method: 采用连续波NV色心系综磁力计，通过频率调制（FM）锁定至光探测磁共振（ODMR）信号的一个侧翼。通过提高软件环路增益G，使离散反馈映射发生翻转（周期倍增）分岔，进入极限环振荡状态。推导了临界增益条件，并进行了可辨识性分析和噪声下的估计器表征。

Result: 推导出临界增益条件Gc=2Dcal/Dtrue，确定了阈值与可测量标量的关系。实验构建了分岔图，观察到临界增益Gc≈2（与自校准环路预测一致），亚阈值临界涨落遵循√(G/(2-G))发散规律。

Conclusion: 通过将锁定系统驱动至分岔点，实现了从锁定放大器到自持振荡器的转变。临界增益条件提供了传感器自校准的便捷途径，亚阈值涨落的发散行为为传感器灵敏度极限提供了新的认知视角。

Abstract: Feedback locking of a probe frequency to a spin resonance is the standard operating mode of precision quantum sensors. Here we deliberately operate such a lock outside its stable regime: a continuous-wave nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensemble magnetometer, frequency-modulation (FM) locked to one flank of its optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR), is driven through the flip (period-doubling) bifurcation of its discrete feedback map by raising the software loop gain $G$. Beyond a critical gain $\Gc$ the lock becomes a self-sustained oscillator whose limit cycle is generated by the loop itself. We derive the threshold condition $\Gc = 2\,\Dcal/\Dtrue$, which identifies the measurable content of the threshold: the ratio of the transduction slope of the ODMR lock-in signal at calibration time $D_{cal}$ to its value at present $D_{true}$. We present an identifiability analysis showing which physical parameters this single scalar can and cannot distinguish, characterize the estimators of $\Gc$ under realistic noise, and report measurements on our current setup: an experimental bifurcation diagram with onset at $\Gc \approx 2$ as predicted for a self-calibrated loop, sub-threshold critical fluctuations following the predicted $\sqrt{G/(2-G)}$ divergence.

</details>

### [118] [Quantum Weyl Relations arising from Two-Term Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22124)
*Qinghua Chen,Yonggang Hu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Dynkin箭图上的二项复形范畴的归一化Hall代数，建立了PBW型分解和混合乘法公式，证明了生成元满足量子Weyl代数关系，构建了Hall-Fock模，并展示了BGP反射对反射子代数的保持作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立二项复形范畴的归一化Hall代数结构，揭示其与量子Weyl代数和Donaldson-Thomas理论中范畴Hall型Weyl作用的联系。

Method: 利用PBW型向量空间分解（Ringel-Hall部分与平移投射部分），推导显式混合乘法公式；通过分析投射模间映射的核与余核确定生成元乘积关系；构造Hall-Fock模实现创建与湮灭算子作用。

Result: 归一化Hall类满足z_i p_i = q^{-1} p_i z_i + 1；每对(p_i,z_i)生成秩一量子Weyl代数，Hom-正交投射族生成高秩量子Weyl子代数；构造了相应的Hall-Fock模；BGP反射保持混合Hall系数。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限域上的Hall代数提供了与DT理论平行的量子Weyl代数模型，推广了Hall代数的乘法结构到混合情形，并建立了反射变换下的不变性。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a Dynkin quiver over $k=\mathbb F_q$, let $A=kQ$, and let $\Ktwo(\cP)$ be the extriangulated category of two-term complexes of projective $A$-modules. We study the square-root normalized Hall algebra of $\Ktwo(\cP)$. We first establish a PBW-type vector-space factorization into the Ringel--Hall part and the shifted-projective part, and derive an explicit mixed multiplication formula. For the indecomposable projectives $P_i$, let $p_i$ and $z_i$ be the normalized Hall classes of $(0\to P_i)$ and $(P_i\to0)$, respectively. We prove \[
  z_ip_i=q^{-1}p_iz_i+1, \] and determine all off-diagonal products $z_ip_j$ in terms of kernels and cokernels of maps $P_i\to P_j$. Hence each pair $(p_i,z_i)$ generates a rank-one quantum Weyl algebra, while every pairwise Hom-orthogonal family of projectives generates a higher-rank quantum Weyl subalgebra. For these subalgebras we construct explicit Hall--Fock modules, on which the $p_i$ act as creation operators and the $z_i$ act as $q$-annihilation operators. This provides a finite-field Hall model parallel to categorical Hall-type Weyl actions in Donaldson--Thomas theory. Finally, we show that BGP reflection transports the corresponding reflection subalgebras and preserves the mixed Hall coefficients.

</details>

### [119] [Mutations of Local Floer Chain Complexes across Hamiltonian Bifurcations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22178)
*Hong-kwon Jo,Dongho Lee*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维哈密顿系统中周期轨道在一般分岔下的局部Floer复形链级变化，通过Floer瀑布计数定理和梯度革命模型，明确了所有七种分岔类型的突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管局部Floer同调在分岔下保持不变，但其底层的链复合体会发生突变——周期轨道及其Conley-Zehnder指标不连续变化，Floer微分必须相应调整以保持同调不变。作者旨在显式计算这些突变，以理解分岔过程中轨道结构的演化。

Method: 1. 证明了自治系统中多重覆盖轨道之间的Floer圆柱的Floer瀑布计数定理（不限于分岔分析）；2. 针对Meyer分类中的每种分岔类型及两种额外的Z₂对称类型，构造了模型哈密顿量，其Floer圆柱是平面梯度轨迹的提升（称为"梯度革命"），将计数问题简化为有限维Morse理论和瀑布计数。

Result: 确定了所有七种分岔类型的显式突变。

Conclusion: 通过Floer瀑布计数定理和梯度革命模型，成功将分岔分析中局部Floer复形的突变计算简化为有限维Morse理论问题，并给出了七种分岔类型的完整分类结果。

Abstract: We compute the chain-level change of local Floer complexes across the generic bifurcations of periodic orbits in four-dimensional Hamiltonian systems. Although local Floer homology is invariant across a bifurcation, the underlying chain complex mutates: periodic orbits and their Conley--Zehnder indices change discontinuously, and the Floer differential must change to compensate. Two tools make this mutation computable. First, we prove a Floer cascade counting theorem for Floer cylinders between multiply covered orbits of an autonomous system, which is not specific to the bifurcation analysis carried out here. Second, for each bifurcation type in Meyer's classification, and for two additional $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric types, we construct a model Hamiltonian whose Floer cylinders are lifts of planar gradient trajectories, which we call the gradient revolutions, reducing the count to finite-dimensional Morse theory and cascade counting. We determine all seven mutations explicitly.

</details>

### [120] [Lifts of partial cohomological field theories and examples of bi-Hamiltonian structures in the non-semisimple case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22084)
*Guido Carlet,Dimitrios Makris,Sergey Shadrin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了部分上同调场论相对于Frobenius代数的提升以及局部多向量场的相应提升，扩展了Della Vedova、Lorenzoni和Savoldi提出的提升过程。这使得我们能够系统地构造具有第二哈密顿结构的非半单齐次部分上同调场论的例子，从而在非半单情形下证实了Buryak等人关于第二泊松括号显式公式的猜想。此外，我们还展示了这些提升构造与Morimoto关于将几何结构提升到与局部代数相关的无穷近点Weil丛的理论之间的一些联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有的提升方法以系统地构造非半单齐次部分上同调场论，这些理论可积系统具有第二哈密顿结构，从而验证Buryak等人关于第二泊松括号显式公式的猜想。

Method: 定义了部分上同调场论相对于Frobenius代数的提升以及局部多向量场的相应提升，扩展了Della Vedova、Lorenzoni和Savoldi提出的提升过程。

Result: 系统地构造了非半单齐次部分上同调场论的例子，其可积系统具有第二哈密顿结构，在非半单情形下证实了Buryak等人的猜想。此外，还建立了这些提升构造与Morimoto关于几何结构提升到Weil丛的理论之间的联系。

Conclusion: 提出的提升方法有效地扩展了已知构造，能够在非半单情形下系统生成具有第二哈密顿结构的部分上同调场论，并与Morimoto的几何提升理论存在深层联系。

Abstract: We define the lift of a partial cohomological field theory with respect to a Frobenius algebra and the corresponding lift of local polyvector fields, extending the lift procedure proposed by Della Vedova, Lorenzoni, and Savoldi. This allows us to systematically produce examples of non-semisimple homogeneous partial cohomological field theories whose integrable systems possess a second Hamiltonian structure, thus confirming the conjecture of Buryak et al. on an explicit formula for the second Poisson bracket in new non-semisimple cases. Moreover we present some relations of these lift constructions with the Morimoto theory of the lift of geometric structures to the Weil bundle of infinitely near points associated to a local algebra.

</details>


### [121] [Probability, Curvature and Spectrum on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21639)
*Tianhong Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为度量图上的拉普拉斯算子建立了迹公式与量子概率、曲率之间的新联系，基于Wilson环和和乐构造图曲率，并运用冯·诺依曼遍历定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为度量图上的迹公式提供全新的量子概率与曲率解释，揭示拉普拉斯算子谱理论与几何曲率之间的深层联系。

Method: 引入基于Wilson线和和乐概念的图曲率定义，结合冯·诺依曼遍历定理，从量子概率角度重新诠释图迹公式。

Result: 成功建立了度量图拉普拉斯算子迹公式与量子概率、曲率之间的新解释框架。

Conclusion: 图迹公式可被赋予量子概率与曲率的几何意义，丰富了度量图谱理论与微分几何的交叉理解。

Abstract: We show that, for a metric graph equipped with the Laplacian operator $Δ=-\frac{d^2}{dx^2}$, the graph trace formula admits a new interpretation in terms of quantum probability and curvature. Our approach is based on a notion of graph curvature inspired by Wilson-lines and holonomy, together with von-Neumann's ergodic theorem.

</details>


### [122] [Nonlinear Schrödinger equation on a closed 3D elastica knot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21750)
*Alain J. Brizard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过变分原理推导了弹性绳结的曲率微分方程，证明了其与非线性薛定谔方程（NLSE）行波解之间的数学等价性，并揭示了封闭弹性绳结对行波解参数空间的拓展要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立弹性绳结（elastica knot）的曲率微分方程，并探究其与非线性薛定谔方程行波解之间的映射关系，从而在几何曲线与非线性波动方程之间建立桥梁。

Method: 基于Frenet-Serret曲率和挠率的变分法推导曲率微分方程；利用Hasimoto变换将空间曲线映射到NLSE；通过行波解形式建立NLSE参数与弹性绳结常数之间的对应关系。

Result: 推导出曲率微分方程 ∂²_s κ = -κ³/2 + k₀⁴τ₀²κ⁻³ + λk₀²κ/2；证明了NLSE行波解可映射为该方程的解，其中 θ′(s_t) = c/(2D) + k₀²τ₀/κ²(s_t)，且弹性绳结常数 k₀²λ = -(c/D)²/2；揭示了封闭3D弹性绳结的行波解需扩展经典参数空间。

Conclusion: NLSE行波解与弹性绳结之间存在严格的数学等价关系，封闭绳结的空间周期性条件对NLSE行波的存在施加了独特约束，并扩展了经典弹性绳结的参数空间。

Abstract: An elastica knot is defined in terms of the Frenet-Serret curvature $κ(s,t)$ as a function of the arclength $s$ along the spatial curve ${\bf r}(s,t)$ at a fixed time $t$, which is a solution of the curvature differential equation $\partial^{2}_{s}κ(s,t) = -\;κ^{3}/2 + k_{0}^{4}τ_{0}^{2}\;κ^{-3} + λ\,k_{0}^{2}κ/2$ that is obtained from a variational principle that minimizes the bending energy of the spatial curve under the constraint of a constant curve length. Here, the Frenet-Serret torsion $τ(s,t)$ satisfies the conservation law $κ^{2}(s,t)\,τ(s,t) \equiv k_{0}^{2}\,τ_{0}$, while $λ$ is a constant of integration. After briefly reviewing the Hasimoto transformation from a space curve ${\bf r}(s,t)$ to the nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLSE) $-\,iD^{-1}\partial_{t}ψ= \partial^{2}_{s}ψ+ \frac{1}{2}\,|ψ|^{2}ψ$, where the constant $D$ has units of fluid circulation (m$^{2}$/sec), we show how the traveling-wave solution $ψ(s,t) = Ψ(s_{t} \equiv s - c\,t) \equiv κ(s_{t})\;\exp[iθ(s_{t})]$ is mapped onto the curvature equation for an elastica knot, with $θ^{\prime}(s_{t}) \equiv c/(2D) + k_{0}^{2}τ_{0}/κ^{2}(s_{t})$ and the elastica-knot constant $k_{0}^{2}λ= -\frac{1}{2}\,(c/D)^{2}$ expressed in terms of the traveling-wave NLSE parameters $(c,D)$. The constraint of a closed 3D elastica knot imposes spatial periodicity conditions that introduce a unique set of knot parameters for which the NLSE traveling wave can exist. The present work shows that the traveling-wave solution on a closed elastica knot requires an extension of the classical elastica-knot parameter space.

</details>


### [123] [Classification of Six-dimensional Real Nilpotent Lie Bialgebras of Symplectic Type and their Poisson-Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22021)
*A. Poursistani,Gh. Haghighatdoost,J. Abedi-Fardad*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对所有六维实幂零李双代数（辛类型）进行了分类，得到了相关六维泊松-李群上的泊松结构，并构造了以泊松-李群为相空间、其对偶李群为对称群的新可积哈密顿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六维实幂零李双代数的辛类型分类，以扩展对低维李双代数结构的理解，并为构造可积哈密顿系统提供基础。

Method: 通过代数分类方法对六维实幂零李双代数按辛类型进行分类，进而导出相关泊松-李群上的泊松结构，并利用对偶李群作为对称群构造可积哈密顿系统。

Result: 完成了所有六维实幂零李双代数（辛类型）的分类，获得了对应泊松-李群上的泊松结构，并得到了一类新的可积哈密顿系统。

Conclusion: 该分类为六维幂零李双代数提供了完整的辛类型分类，所构造的泊松结构和可积哈密顿系统丰富了该领域的理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we classify all six-dimensional real nilpotent Lie bialgebras of symplectic type. The Poisson structures on all of the related six-dimensional Poisson-Lie groups are obtained. Some new integrable Hamiltonian systems for which the Poisson-Lie group plays the role of a phase space and its dual Lie group plays the role of a symmetry group of the system are obtained.

</details>


### [124] [Self-adjoint extensions of $k$-photon light-matter Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22378)
*Felix Fischer,Felix Knapp,Daniel Burgarth,Davide Lonigro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多光子光-物质相互作用模型的自伴性。当耦合算符Σ是正规且非零时，哈密顿量H在k≤2时是自伴的，k≥3时非自伴并给出了亏指数、自伴延拓参数化及离散谱条件。Σ的正规性条件是最优的，Jaynes-Cummings模型对任意k都是自伴的。结果在k-光子Rabi和Dicke模型上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子相互作用是量子光学中非线性现象的重要来源，但k≥3的高阶多光子过程在数学上可能导致哈密顿量非自伴，进而影响物理系统的可观测性。本文旨在严格刻画这类模型的自伴性条件，为实验上可实现的非线性量子光学系统提供数学基础。

Method: 采用块Jacobi分解结合依赖于Σ极分解的酉变换，将无穷维算子的自伴性问题转化为有限维矩阵问题进行分析。通过亏格理论计算亏指数，并对自伴延拓进行分类。

Result: (1) 当Σ是正规且非零时，H自伴当且仅当k≤2；(2) k≥3时亏指数为(∞,∞)，给出了所有自伴延拓的完整参数化；(3) 若物质系统有限维，每个自伴延拓都有纯离散谱；(4) Σ的正规性条件是最优的——k-光子Jaynes-Cummings模型（非正规耦合）对任意k都是自伴的。

Conclusion: 严格证明了高阶多光子（k≥3）Rabi/Dicke模型在正规耦合下本质非自伴，必须通过自伴延拓才能获得物理上有意义的量子演化。这为实验上可实现的非线性量子光学模型提供了严格的数学框架，并指出了正规性条件在自伴性中的关键作用。

Abstract: Multiphoton light-matter interactions, in which a bosonic mode exchanges $k$ excitations at a time with a quantum system, are a source of genuine nonlinearity in quantum optics and are increasingly accessible experimentally. Here we study the class of operators $H = H_{\rm mat}\otimes I + I\otimesωa^\ast a + Σ\otimes(a^\ast)^k + Σ^\ast\otimes a^k$ on $\mathcal{H}\otimes L^2(\mathbb{R})$, coupling a single bosonic mode to an arbitrary matter system through a bounded operator $Σ$. When $Σ$ is normal and nonzero, we prove that $H$ is self-adjoint if and only if $k\leq2$; for $k\geq3$ we compute the deficiency indices, parametrise all self-adjoint extensions, and show that every extension has purely discrete spectrum whenever the matter system is finite-dimensional. Our analysis rests on a block Jacobi decomposition paired with a suitable unitary transformation depending on the polar decomposition of $Σ$. The normality of $Σ$ is optimal: a $k$-photon Jaynes-Cummings model, with non-normal coupling, remains self-adjoint for every $k$. We illustrate our results on the $k$-photon Rabi and Dicke models.

</details>


### [125] [Essential self-adjointness of semi-bounded operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22472)
*M. Griesemer,V. Kußmaul*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半有界算子本质自伴性的局部化判定准则，该准则将Wienholtz-Simader方法推广到抽象算子理论框架，并成功应用于多粒子薛定谔算子、赝相对论哈密顿量及非相对论量子电动力学标准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Wienholtz-Simader方法在处理半有界椭圆偏微分算子的本质自伴性时效果显著，但缺乏统一的抽象算子理论框架。本文旨在建立一个更一般的抽象框架，以推广该方法并解决更广泛的一类算子（如多粒子系统、赝相对论模型及QED模型）的本质自伴性问题。

Method: 利用半有界算子（下界为1）在伴随核平凡时本质自伴的性质，进一步证明若伴随核属于Friedrichs延拓的型域，则本质自伴性成立。通过引入按范数逼近恒等算子的有界算子族来定义局部性，从而建立抽象算子理论框架，将Wienholtz-Simader方法推广到一般情形。

Result: 获得了多粒子薛定谔算子、赝相对论哈密顿量以及非相对论量子电动力学标准模型中关于本质自伴性的竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 本文发展的抽象算子理论框架成功推广了Wienholtz-Simader方法，为一大类半有界算子的本质自伴性分析提供了统一工具，并在多个重要物理模型中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: A semi-bounded operator that is bounded below by one is essentially self-adjoint if the kernel of the adjoint is trivial. This is the case if the kernel of the adjoint belongs to the form domain of the Friedrichs extension. We describe a local version of this criterion, where locality is defined in terms of bounded operators approaching the identity. The result is an abstract operator theoretic framework that generalizes the method by Wienholtz and Simader developed in the context of semi-bounded elliptic partial differential operators. As applications, we obtain competitive results on essential self-adjointness of many-particle Schrödinger operators, pseudo-relativistic Hamiltonians, and the standard model of non-relativistic quantum electrodynamics.

</details>
